I'll solve this step by step.

## Problem Understanding

We need to simulate how passengers from 4 different countries sit in a row of chairs according to their specific sitting preferences:

1. **Country A**: Sit at the left end (first empty chair from left)
2. **Country B**: Sit at the right end (first empty chair from right), except if only the chair next to A is empty, then sit at the left end
3. **Country C**: Wants to sit next to someone. If possible, sit to the right of the leftmost person; if not, sit to the left of that person; if not, sit next to the next person; if no one is sitting, sit in the middle
4. **Country D**: Doesn't want to sit next to someone. Sit in the chair closest to the nearest occupied chair (minimizing distance), and if multiple options, choose the leftmost one. If no one is sitting, sit in the leftmost chair

## Step-by-Step Solution

### Step 1: Parse the input and handle multiple datasets
- Read n (chairs) and m (passengers)
- Continue until we get "0 0"
- For each dataset, process m passengers in order

### Step 2: Implement each country's sitting rule
- Track current seating status (which chairs are occupied)
- For each passenger, apply their specific rule

### Step 3: Handle the seating logic carefully
- For A: find first empty chair from left
- For B: find first empty chair from right, but if only chair next to A is empty, sit at left end
- For C: try to sit adjacent to leftmost person, then next person, then middle if needed
- For D: find chair with minimum distance to nearest occupied chair, leftmost if tied, or leftmost if empty

Let me implement this:

```python
def solve():
    while True:
        line = input().strip()
        if line == "0 0":
            break
            
        n, m = map(int, line.split())
        passengers = []
        for _ in range(m):
            passengers.append(input().strip())
        
        # Initialize chairs as empty (represented by True for occupied, False for empty)
        chairs = [False] * n  # False = empty, True = occupied
        
        # Process each passenger in order
        for passenger in passengers:
            if passenger == 'A':
                # Sit at left end (first empty chair from left)
                for i in range(n):
                    if not chairs[i]:
                        chairs[i] = True
                        break
                        
            elif passenger == 'B':
                # Sit at right end (first empty chair from right), 
                # except if only the chair next to A is empty, then sit at left end
                # First find if there's a person from A already seated
                has_a = False
                a_position = -1
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i]:
                        if passenger == 'A' or (passenger == 'B' and i == a_position):
                            has_a = True
                            a_position = i
                            break
                
                # Check if there's a specific chair next to A that's empty
                # Actually, let's re-read: "if only the person next to Country A is vacant, be patient and sit in the vacant chair from the left end"
                # This seems to mean: if the only empty chair adjacent to A is available, sit at left end
                # But the description is confusing. Looking at examples...
                
                # Simpler interpretation: sit at right end, but if the only empty chair adjacent to A is available, sit at left end
                # Actually, let's check the pattern: B should avoid sitting next to A
                
                # Alternative interpretation: try to sit at right end, but if the chair next to A is empty and it's the only empty chair, then sit at left end
                # Let's implement: first try to sit at right end, but check if that position is adjacent to A
                
                # Find positions of all A's
                a_positions = []
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i]:
                        if passenger == 'A' or (passenger == 'B' and i == a_position):
                            a_positions.append(i)
                
                # Find the rightmost empty chair
                rightmost_empty = -1
                for i in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                    if not chairs[i]:
                        rightmost_empty = i
                        break
                
                # Find the leftmost empty chair
                leftmost_empty = -1
                for i in range(n):
                    if not chairs[i]:
                        leftmost_empty = i
                        break
                
                # Check if there's an A already sitting
                has_a = False
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i]:
                        has_a = True
                        break
                
                # If there's no A, just sit at right end
                if not has_a:
                    chairs[rightmost_empty] = True
                else:
                    # Check if the chair next to A (from right) is empty
                    # Try to sit at right end
                    right_choice = -1
                    for i in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                        if not chairs[i]:
                            right_choice = i
                            break
                    
                    # Check if any chair adjacent to A is empty
                    a_adjacent_empty = False
                    for i in range(n):
                        if chairs[i]:  # This is an A position
                            # Check if left neighbor is empty
                            if i-1 >= 0 and not chairs[i-1]:
                                a_adjacent_empty = True
                            # Check if right neighbor is empty
                            if i+1 < n and not chairs[i+1]:
                                a_adjacent_empty = True
                    
                    # If there's an A and there's an empty chair adjacent to A, sit at right end
                    # Otherwise, sit at left end
                    # But the problem says: "if only the person next to Country A is vacant, be patient and sit in the vacant chair from the left end"
                    if a_adjacent_empty:
                        # Sit at right end
                        if right_choice != -1:
                            chairs[right_choice] = True
                    else:
                        # Sit at left end
                        if leftmost_empty != -1:
                            chairs[leftmost_empty] = True
                            
            elif passenger == 'C':
                # C wants to sit next to someone
                # Look from left side, try to sit to the right of the person sitting on the far left
                # If full, try to sit to the left of that person
                # If also filled, try to sit next to the next person
                # If no one is sitting, sit in middle (n/2 + 1 if odd, (n+1)/2)
                
                # Find leftmost occupied chair
                leftmost_occupied = -1
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i]:
                        leftmost_occupied = i
                        break
                
                if leftmost_occupied == -1:
                    # No one sitting, sit in middle
                    if n % 2 == 1:
                        chairs[n//2] = True
                    else:
                        chairs[n//2] = True
                else:
                    # Try to sit to the right of leftmost_occupied
                    right_of_leftmost = leftmost_occupied + 1
                    if right_of_leftmost < n and not chairs[right_of_leftmost]:
                        chairs[right_of_leftmost] = True
                    else:
                        # Try to sit to the left of leftmost_occupied
                        left_of_leftmost = leftmost_occupied - 1
                        if left_of_leftmost >= 0 and not chairs[left_of_leftmost]:
                            chairs[left_of_leftmost] = True
                        else:
                            # Try to sit next to the next person (which would be next to the next occupied chair)
                            # Find next occupied chair after leftmost_occupied
                            next_occupied = leftmost_occupied + 1
                            while next_occupied < n and chairs[next_occupied]:
                                next_occupied += 1
                            
                            if next_occupied < n:
                                # Try to sit to the right of next_occupied
                                right_of_next = next_occupied + 1
                                if right_of_next < n and not chairs[right_of_next]:
                                    chairs[right_of_next] = True
                                else:
                                    # Try to sit to the left of next_occupied
                                    left_of_next = next_occupied - 1
                                    if left_of_next >= 0 and not chairs[left_of_next]:
                                        chairs[left_of_next] = True
                                    else:
                                        # If all else fails, sit in middle
                                        if n % 2 == 1:
                                            chairs[n//2] = True
                                        else:
                                            chairs[n//2] = True
                            else:
                                # If no next occupied chair, sit in middle
                                if n % 2 == 1:
                                    chairs[n//2] = True
                                else:

def solve():
    while True:
        line = input().strip()
        if line == "0 0":
            break
            
        n, m = map(int, line.split())
        passengers = []
        for _ in range(m):
            passengers.append(input().strip())
        
        # Initialize chairs as empty
        chairs = [False] * n  # False = empty, True = occupied
        
        # Process each passenger in order
        for i, passenger in enumerate(passengers):
            if passenger == 'A':
                # Sit at left end (first empty chair from left)
                for j in range(n):
                    if not chairs[j]:
                        chairs[j] = True
                        break
                        
            elif passenger == 'B':
                # Sit at right end (first empty chair from right)
                # But if only the chair next to A is vacant, sit at left end
                # First, check if there's any A already sitting
                has_a = False
                a_position = -1
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j]:
                        if passenger == 'A':
                            has_a = True
                            a_position = j
                        # We need to track all A positions
                # Actually, let's re-read: "if only the person next to Country A is vacant, be patient and sit in the vacant chair from the left end"
                # This means: if the only empty chair adjacent to an A is empty, then sit at left end
                
                # Find all A positions
                a_positions = []
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j]:
                        if passenger == 'A' or (passenger == 'B' and j == a_position):
                            a_positions.append(j)
                
                # Find the rightmost empty chair
                rightmost_empty = -1
                for j in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                    if not chairs[j]:
                        rightmost_empty = j
                        break
                
                # Check if there's an A and if there's an empty chair adjacent to it
                has_a = False
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j]:
                        has_a = True
                        # Check if left neighbor is empty or right neighbor is empty
                        if j-1 >= 0 and not chairs[j-1]:
                            # Empty chair to left of A
                            pass
                        if j+1 < n and not chairs[j+1]:
                            # Empty chair to right of A
                            pass
                
                # If there's at least one A and there's an empty chair adjacent to A, sit at right end
                # Otherwise, sit at left end
                leftmost_empty = -1
                for j in range(n):
                    if not chairs[j]:
                        leftmost_empty = j
                        break
                
                if not has_a:
                    # No A, sit at right end
                    if rightmost_empty != -1:
                        chairs[rightmost_empty] = True
                else:
                    # Check if there's any empty chair adjacent to A
                    has_adjacent_empty = False
                    for j in range(n):
                        if chairs[j]:
                            if j-1 >= 0 and not chairs[j-1]:
                                has_adjacent_empty = True
                            if j+1 < n and not chairs[j+1]:
                                has_adjacent_empty = True
                    
                    if has_adjacent_empty:
                        # Sit at right end
                        if rightmost_empty != -1:
                            chairs[rightmost_empty] = True
                    else:
                        # Sit at left end
                        if leftmost_empty != -1:
                            chairs[leftmost_empty] = True
                            
            elif passenger == 'C':
                # C wants to sit next to someone
                # Look from the person sitting on the far left, try to sit to the right of that person
                # If full, try to sit to the left of that person
                # If also filled, try to sit next to the next person under the same conditions
                # If no one is sitting, sit in the middle
                
                # Find the leftmost occupied chair
                leftmost_occupied = -1
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j]:
                        leftmost_occupied = j
                        break
                
                if leftmost_occupied == -1:
                    # No one sitting, sit in middle
                    if n % 2 == 1:
                        chairs[n//2] = True
                    else:
                        chairs[n//2] = True
                else:
                    # Try to sit to the right of leftmost_occupied
                    right_pos = leftmost_occupied + 1
                    if right_pos < n and not chairs[right_pos]:
                        chairs[right_pos] = True
                    else:
                        # Try to sit to the left of leftmost_occupied
                        left_pos = leftmost_occupied - 1
                        if left_pos >= 0 and not chairs[left_pos]:
                            chairs[left_pos] = True
                        else:
                            # Try to sit next to the next occupied person
                            next_occupied = leftmost_occupied + 1
                            while next_occupied < n and chairs[next_occupied]:
                                next_occupied += 1
                            
                            if next_occupied < n:
                                # Try to sit to the right of next_occupied
                                right_of_next = next_occupied + 1
                                if right_of_next < n and not chairs[right_of_next]:
                                    chairs[right_of_next] = True
                                else:
                                    # Try to sit to the left of next_occupied
                                    left_of_next = next_occupied - 1
                                    if left_of_next >= 0 and not chairs[left_of_next]:
                                        chairs[left_of_next] = True
                                    else:
                                        # If all else fails, sit in middle
                                        if n % 2 == 1:
                                            chairs[n//2] = True
                                        else:
                                            chairs[n//2] = True
                            else:
                                # If no next occupied chair, sit in middle
                                if n % 2 == 1:
                                    chairs[n//2] = True
                                else:
                                    chairs[n//2] = True
                                
            elif passenger == 'D':
                # D doesn't want to sit next to someone
                # Sitting in the chair that is closest to the closest occupied person
                # If multiple choices with same distance, choose leftmost
                # If no one is sitting, sit in leftmost
                
                # First, check if there are any occupied chairs
                has_occupied = False
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j]:
                        has_occupied = True
                        break
                
                if not has_occupied:
                    # No one sitting, sit in leftmost
                    chairs[0] = True
                else:
                    # Find the empty chair with minimum distance to nearest occupied chair
                    min_dist = float('inf')
                    best_pos = -1
                    
                    for i in range(n):
                        if not chairs[i]:
                            # Find distance to nearest occupied chair
                            dist = float('inf')
                            for j in range(n):
                                if chairs[j]:
                                    d = abs(i - j)
                                    dist = min(dist, d)
                            
                            if dist < min_dist:
                                min_dist = dist
                                best_pos = i
                            elif dist == min_dist and i < best_pos:
                                best_pos = i
                    
                    chairs[best_pos] = True
        
        # Convert to output string (empty chairs are '#')
        result = []
        for i in range(n):
            if chairs[i]:
                # We need to know which passenger sat here
                # We'll need to track this with a different approach
                pass
        
        # Let me entirely restart with a clean implementation that tracks who sits where
        
def solve_clean():
    while True:
        line = input().strip()
        if line == "0 0":
            break
            
        n, m = map(int, line.split())
        passengers = []
        for _ in range(m):
            passengers.append(input().strip())
        
        # Track which chairs are occupied and by whom
        # chairs[i] = nationality if occupied, None if empty
        chairs = [None] * n
        
        for i, p in enumerate(passengers):
            if p == 'A':
                # Sit at left end (first empty from left)
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] is None:
                        chairs[j] = p
                        break
                        
            elif p == 'B':
                # Sit at right end (first empty from right)
                # Except if only the chair next to A is vacant, then sit at left end
                
                # First, find if there's any A already seated
                has_a = False
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] == 'A':
                        has_a = True
                        break
                
                # Find the rightmost empty chair
                rightmost_empty = -1
                for j in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                    if chairs[j] is None:
                        rightmost_empty = j
                        break
                
                # Find the leftmost empty chair
                leftmost_empty = -1
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] is None:
                        leftmost_empty = j
                        break
                
                # Check if there's an empty chair adjacent to any A
                has_adjacent_empty = False
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] == 'A':
                        if j-1 >= 0 and chairs[j-1] is None:
                            has_adjacent_empty = True
                        if j+1 < n and chairs[j+1] is None:
                            has_adjacent_empty = True
                
                # If there's an A and there's an empty chair adjacent to it, sit at right end
                # Otherwise, sit at left end
                if has_a and has_adjacent_empty:
                    if rightmost_empty != -1:
                        chairs[rightmost_empty] = p
                else:
                    if leftmost_empty != -1:
                        chairs[leftmost_empty] = p
                        
            elif p == 'C':
                # C wants to sit next to someone
                # Try to sit to the right of the leftmost occupied person
                # If full, try to sit to the left of that person
                # If full, try to sit next to next person
                # If no one sitting, sit in middle
                
                leftmost_occupied = -1
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] is not None:
                        leftmost_occupied = j
                        break
                
                if leftmost_occupied == -1:
                    # No one sitting, sit in middle
                    if n % 2 == 1:
                        chairs[n//2] = p
                    else:
                        chairs[n//2] = p
                else:
                    # Try to sit to the right of leftmost_occupied
                    right_pos = leftmost_occupied + 1
                    if right_pos < n and chairs[right_pos] is None:
                        chairs[right_pos] = p
                    else:
                        # Try to sit to the left of leftmost_occupied
                        left_pos = leftmost_occupied - 1
                        if left_pos >= 0 and chairs[left_pos] is None:
                            chairs[left_pos] = p
                        else:
                            # Try to sit next to the next occupied person
                            next_occupied = leftmost_occupied + 1
                            while next_occupied < n and chairs[next_occupied] is not None:
                                next_occupied += 1
                            
                            if next_occupied < n:
                                # Try to sit to the right of next_occupied
                                right_of_next = next_occupied + 1
                                if right_of_next < n and chairs[right_of_next] is None:
                                    chairs[right_of_next] = p
                                else:
                                    # Try to sit to the left of next_occupied
                                    left_of_next = next_occupied - 1
                                    if left_of_next >= 0 and chairs[left_of_next] is None:
                                        chairs[left_of_next] = p
                                    else:
                                        # All else fails, sit in middle
                                        if n % 2 == 1:
                                            chairs[n//2] = p
                                        else:
                                            chairs[n//2] = p
                            else:
                                # All else fails, sit in middle
                                if n % 2 == 1:
                                    chairs[n//2] = p
                                else:
                                    chairs[n//2] = p
                                
            elif p == 'D':
                # D doesn't want to sit next to someone
                # Sit in the chair closest to the nearest occupied person
                # If tie, choose leftmost
                # If no one sitting, sit in leftmost
                
                has_occupied = False
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] is not None:
                        has_occupied = True
                        break
                
                if not has_occupied:
                    # No one sitting, sit in leftmost
                    chairs[0] = p
                else:
                    # Find the empty chair with minimum distance to nearest occupied chair
                    min_dist = float('inf')
                    best_pos = -1
                    
                    for i in range(n):
                        if chairs[i] is None:
                            # Find distance to nearest occupied chair
                            dist = float('inf')
                            for j in range(n):
                                if chairs[j] is not None:
                                    d = abs(i - j)
                                    dist = min(dist, d)
                            
                            if dist < min_dist:
                                min_dist = dist
                                best_pos = i
                            elif dist == min_dist and i < best_pos:
                                best_pos = i
                    
                    chairs[best_pos] = p
        
        # Convert to output string
        output = ""
        for i in range(n):
            if chairs[i] is None:
                output += "#"
            else:
                output += chairs[i]
        
        print(output)

solve_clean()
```

def solve():
    while True:
        line = input().strip()
        if line == "0 0":
            break
            
        n, m = map(int, line.split())
        passengers = []
        for _ in range(m):
            passengers.append(input().strip())
        
        # Initialize chairs as empty (represented by True for occupied, False for empty)
        chairs = [False] * n  # False = empty, True = occupied
        
        # Process each passenger in order
        for passenger in passengers:
            if passenger == 'A':
                # Sit at left end (first empty chair from left)
                for i in range(n):
                    if not chairs[i]:
                        chairs[i] = True
                        break
                        
            elif passenger == 'B':
                # Sit at right end (first empty chair from right), 
                # except if only the chair next to A is empty, then sit at left end
                # First find if there's a person from A already seated
                has_a = False
                a_position = -1
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i]:
                        if passenger == 'A' or (passenger == 'B' and i == a_position):
                            has_a = True
                            a_position = i
                            break
                
                # Check if there's a specific chair next to A that's empty
                # Actually, let's re-read: "if only the person next to Country A is vacant, be patient and sit in the vacant chair from the left end"
                # This seems to mean: if the only empty chair adjacent to A is available, sit at left end
                # But the description is confusing. Looking at examples...
                
                # Simpler interpretation: sit at right end, but if the only empty chair adjacent to A is available, sit at left end
                # Actually, let's check the pattern: B should avoid sitting next to A
                
                # Alternative interpretation: try to sit at right end, but if the chair next to A is empty and it's the only empty chair, then sit at left end
                # Let's implement: first try to sit at right end, but check if that position is adjacent to A
                
                # Find positions of all A's
                a_positions = []
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i]:
                        if passenger == 'A' or (passenger == 'B' and i == a_position):
                            a_positions.append(i)
                
                # Find the rightmost empty chair
                rightmost_empty = -1

                    if not chairs[i]:
                        rightmost_empty = i
                        break
                
                # Find the leftmost empty chair
                leftmost_empty = -1
                for i in range(n):
                    if not chairs[i]:
                        leftmost_empty = i
                        break
                
                # Check if there's an A already sitting
                has_a = False
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i]:
                        has_a = True
                        break
                
                # If there's no A, just sit at right end
                if not has_a:
                    chairs[rightmost_empty] = True
                else:
                    # Check if the chair next to A (from right) is empty
                    # Try to sit at right end
                    right_choice = -1
                    for i in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                        if not chairs[i]:
                            right_choice = i
                            break
                    
                    # Check if any chair adjacent to A is empty
                    a_adjacent_empty = False
                    for i in range(n):
                        if chairs[i]:  # This is an A position
                            # Check if left neighbor is empty
                            if i-1 >= 0 and not chairs[i-1]:
                                a_adjacent_empty = True
                            # Check if right neighbor is empty
                            if i+1 < n and not chairs[i+1]:
                                a_adjacent_empty = True
                    
                    # If there's an A and there's an empty chair adjacent to A, sit at right end
                    # Otherwise, sit at left end
                    # But the problem says: "if only the person next to Country A is vacant, be patient and sit in the vacant chair from the left end"
                    if a_adjacent_empty:
                        # Sit at right end
                        if right_choice != -1:
                            chairs[right_choice] = True
                    else:
                        # Sit at left end
                        if leftmost_empty != -1:
                            chairs[leftmost_empty] = True
                            
            elif passenger == 'C':
                # C wants to sit next to someone
                # Look from left side, try to sit to the right of the person sitting on the far left
                # If full, try to sit to the left of that person
                # If also filled, try to sit next to the next person
                # If no one is sitting, sit in middle (n/2 + 1 if odd, (n+1)/2)
                
                # Find leftmost occupied chair
                leftmost_occupied = -1
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i]:
                        leftmost_occupied = i
                        break
                
                if leftmost_occupied == -1:

                    if n % 2 == 1:
                        chairs[n//2] = True
                    else:
                        chairs[n//2] = True
                else:

                    right_of_leftmost = leftmost_occupied + 1
                    if right_of_leftmost < n and not chairs[right_of_leftmost]:
                        chairs[right_of_leftmost] = True
                    else:
                        # Try to sit to the left of leftmost_occupied
                        left_of_leftmost = leftmost_occupied - 1
                        if left_of_leftmost >= 0 and not chairs[left_of_leftmost]:
                            chairs[left_of_leftmost] = True
                        else:
                            # Try to sit next to the next person (which would be next to the next occupied chair)
                            # Find next occupied chair after leftmost_occupied
                            next_occupied = leftmost_occupied + 1

                        has_occupied = True
                        break

                            while next_occupied < n and chairs[next_occupied]:
                                next_occupied += 1
                            
                            if next_occupied < n:
                                # Try to sit to the right of next_occupied
                                right_of_next = next_occupied + 1
                                if right_of_next < n and not chairs[right_of_next]:
                                    chairs[right_of_next] = True
                                else:
                                    # Try to sit to the left of next_occupied
                                    left_of_next = next_occupied - 1
                                    if left_of_next >= 0 and not chairs[left_of_next]:
                                        chairs[left_of_next] = True
                                    else:
                                        # If all else fails, sit in middle
                                        if n % 2 == 1:
                                            chairs[n//2] = True
                                        else:
                                            chairs[n//2] = True
                            else:
                                # If no next occupied chair, sit in middle
                                if n % 2 == 1:
                                    chairs[n//2] = True
                                else:
                                    chairs[n//2] = True
                                
            elif passenger == 'D':
                # D doesn't want to sit next to someone
                # Sitting in the chair that is closest to the closest occupied person
                # If multiple choices with same distance, choose leftmost
                # If no one is sitting, sit in leftmost
                
                # First, check if there are any occupied chairs
                has_occupied = False
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j]:
                        has_occupied = True
                        break
                
                if not has_occupied:
                    # No one sitting, sit in leftmost
                    chairs[0] = True
                else:
                    # Find the empty chair with minimum distance to nearest occupied chair
                    min_dist = float('inf')
                    best_pos = -1
                    
                    for i in range(n):
                        if not chairs[i]:
                            # Find distance to nearest occupied chair
                            dist = float('inf')
                            for j in range(n):
                                if chairs[j]:
                                    d = abs(i - j)
                                    dist = min(dist, d)
                            
                            if dist < min_dist:
                                min_dist = dist
                                best_pos = i
                            elif dist == min_dist and i < best_pos:
                                best_pos = i
                    
                    chairs[best_pos] = True
        
        # Convert to output string (empty chairs are '#')
        result = []
        for i in range(n):
            if chairs[i]:
                # We need to know which passenger sat here
                # We'll need to track this with a different approach
                pass
        
        # Let me entirely restart with a clean implementation that tracks who sits where
        
def solve_clean():
    while True:
        line = input().strip()
        if line == "0 0":
            break
            
        n, m = map(int, line.split())
        passengers = []
        for _ in range(m):
            passengers.append(input().strip())
        
        # Track which chairs are occupied and by whom
        # chairs[i] = nationality if occupied, None if empty
        chairs = [None] * n
        
        for i, p in enumerate(passengers):
            if p == 'A':
                # Sit at left end (first empty from left)
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] is None:
                        chairs[j] = p
                        break
                        
            elif p == 'B':
                # Sit at right end (first empty from right)
                # Except if only the chair next to A is vacant, then sit at left end
                
                # First, find if there's any A already seated
                has_a = False
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] == 'A':
                        has_a = True
                        break
                
                # Find the rightmost empty chair
                rightmost_empty = -1
                for j in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                    if chairs[j] is None:
                        rightmost_empty = j
                        break
                
                # Find the leftmost empty chair
                leftmost_empty = -1
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] is None:
                        leftmost_empty = j
                        break
                
                # Check if there's an empty chair adjacent to any A
                has_adjacent_empty = False
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] == 'A':
                        if j-1 >= 0 and chairs[j-1] is None:
                            has_adjacent_empty = True
                        if j+1 < n and chairs[j+1] is None:
                            has_adjacent_empty = True
                
                # If there's an A and there's an empty chair adjacent to it, sit at right end
                # Otherwise, sit at left end
                if has_a and has_adjacent_empty:
                    if rightmost_empty != -1:
                        chairs[rightmost_empty] = p
                else:
                    if leftmost_empty != -1:
                        chairs[leftmost_empty] = p
                        
            elif p == 'C':
                # C wants to sit next to someone
                # Try to sit to the right of the leftmost occupied person
                # If full, try to sit to the left of that person
                # If full, try to sit next to next person
                # If no one sitting, sit in middle
                
                leftmost_occupied = -1
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] is not None:
                        leftmost_occupied = j
                        break
                
                if leftmost_occupied == -1:
                    # No one sitting, sit in middle
                    if n % 2 == 1:
                        chairs[n//2] = p
                    else:
                        chairs[n//2] = p
                else:
                    # Try to sit to the right of leftmost_occupied
                    right_pos = leftmost_occupied + 1
                    if right_pos < n and chairs[right_pos] is None:
                        chairs[right_pos] = p
                    else:
                        # Try to sit to the left of leftmost_occupied
                        left_pos = leftmost_occupied - 1
                        if left_pos >= 0 and chairs[left_pos] is None:
                            chairs[left_pos] = p
                        else:
                            # Try to sit next to the next occupied person
                            next_occupied = leftmost_occupied + 1
                            while next_occupied < n and chairs[next_occupied] is not None:
                                next_occupied += 1
                            
                            if next_occupied < n:
                                # Try to sit to the right of next_occupied
                                right_of_next = next_occupied + 1
                                if right_of_next < n and chairs[right_of_next] is None:
                                    chairs[right_of_next] = p
                                else:
                                    # Try to sit to the left of next_occupied
                                    left_of_next = next_occupied - 1
                                    if left_of_next >= 0 and chairs[left_of_next] is None:
                                        chairs[left_of_next] = p
                                    else:
                                        # All else fails, sit in middle
                                        if n % 2 == 1:
                                            chairs[n//2] = p
                                        else:
                                            chairs[n//2] = p
                            else:
                                # All else fails, sit in middle
                                if n % 2 == 1:
                                    chairs[n//2] = p
                                else:
                                    chairs[n//2] = p
                                
            elif p == 'D':
                # D doesn't want to sit next to someone
                # Sit in the chair closest to the nearest occupied person
                # If tie, choose leftmost
                # If no one sitting, sit in leftmost
                
                has_occupied = False
                for j in range(n):
                    if chairs[j] is not None:
                        has_occupied = True
                        break
                
                if not has_occupied:
                    # No one sitting, sit in leftmost
                    chairs[0] = p
                else:
                    # Find the empty chair with minimum distance to nearest occupied chair
                    min_dist = float('inf')
                    best_pos = -1
                    
                    for i in range(n):
                        if chairs[i] is None:
                            # Find distance to nearest occupied chair
                            dist = float('inf')
                            for j in range(n):
                                if chairs[j] is not None:
                                    d = abs(i - j)
                                    dist = min(dist, d)
                            
                            if dist < min_dist:
                                min_dist = dist
                                best_pos = i
                            elif dist == min_dist and i < best_pos:
                                best_pos = i
                    
                    chairs[best_pos] = p
        
        # Convert to output string
        output = ""
        for i in range(n):
            if chairs[i] is None:
                output += "#"
            else:
                output += chairs[i]
        
        print(output)

## Implementation Plan

1. For each passenger in order:
   - A: sit at leftmost empty chair
   - B: sit at rightmost empty chair, unless the chair immediately next to A is empty (i.e., if A is at position p, then position p+1 is empty), in which case sit at leftmost empty chair
   - C: try to sit next to someone. Start with leftmost person (say at position p). Try right of p, then left of p, then next person's right, then next person's left, etc. If none available, sit in middle (n//2 + 1 if odd, (n+1)//2 if odd)
   - D: find the position with minimum distance to any occupied seat. If multiple with same distance, choose leftmost. If the minimum distance is 1 (adjacent), then sit at leftmost such adjacent position. Otherwise, sit at the position with minimum distance.

But the problem says D doesn't want to sit next to someone, so maybe D only considers non-adjacent seats.

Given the examples, I'll implement based on the expected outputs.

Looking at both examples, I think for D in second example, D sits at 0, C at 1, then B sits at 2, A at 3, and position 4 is empty. But the output is "DCA#B", which has C at position 1, A at position 2.

I think I need to implement exactly as per the rules.

Let me implement based on the description:

```python
def solve():
    while True:
        line = input().strip()
        if line == "0 0":
            break
            
        n, m = map(int, line.split())
        passengers = []
        for _ in range(m):
            passengers.append(input().strip())
        
        # chairs: 0 to n-1, initially all empty
        chairs = ['#'] * n
        
        for country in passengers:
            if country == 'A':
                # Sit at leftmost empty chair
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] == '#':
                        chairs[i] = 'A'
                        break
            elif country == 'B':
                # Sit at rightmost empty chair, unless the chair next to A is vacant
                # First, check if there's an A already - if yes, check if the chair next to A is empty
                # If the next to A chair is empty, sit at leftmost empty chair
                # Otherwise sit at rightmost empty chair
                
                # Find if there's any A
                has_A = False
                a_pos = -1
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] == 'A':
                        has_A = True
                        a_pos = i
                        break
                
                # Find all empty chairs
                empty_chairs = []
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] == '#':
                        empty_chairs.append(i)
                
                if has_A and a_pos != -1:
                    # Check if the chair immediately next to A is empty
                    # Only check right next to A (since left might be out of bound)
                    right_of_A = a_pos + 1
                    if right_of_A < n and chairs[right_of_A] == '#':
                        # If right of A is empty, sit at leftmost empty chair
                        for i in range(n):
                            if chairs[i] == '#':
                                chairs[i] = 'B'
                                break
                    else:
                        # Sit at rightmost empty chair
                        for i in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                            if chairs[i] == '#':
                                chairs[i] = 'B'
                                break
                else:
                    # No A, so just sit at rightmost empty chair
                    for i in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                        if chairs[i] == '#':
                            chairs[i] = 'B'
                            break
            elif country == 'C':
                # Try to sit next to someone
                # Look from left side, try to sit to the right of the person sitting on the far left
                # If full, try to sit to the left of that person
                # If also filled, try with next person
                # If no one is sitting, sit in middle
                
                # Find all occupied positions
                occupied = []
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] != '#':
                        occupied.append(i)
                
                if len(occupied) == 0:
                    # No one sitting, sit in middle
                    if n % 2 == 1:
                        middle = n // 2 + 1
                        if middle < n:
                            chairs[middle - 1] = 'C'
                    else:
                        middle = (n + 1) // 2
                        if middle < n:
                            chairs[middle - 1] = 'C'
                else:
                    # Find the leftmost occupied position
                    leftmost_occupied = min(occupied)
                    
                    # Try to sit to the right of leftmost_occupied
                    right_pos = leftmost_occupied + 1
                    if right_pos < n and chairs[right_pos] == '#':
                        chairs[right_pos] = 'C'
                    # Try to sit to the left of leftmost_occupied
                    elif leftmost_occupied > 0 and chairs[leftmost_occupied - 1] == '#':
                        chairs[leftmost_occupied - 1] = 'C'
                    # Try with next person (to the right of leftmost_occupied)
                    else:
                        # Find next person (next to the right of leftmost)
                        next_person = None
                        for pos in occupied:
                            if pos > leftmost_occupied:
                                next_person = pos
                                break
                        if next_person is not None:
                            # Try to sit to the right of next_person
                            right_of_next = next_person + 1
                            if right_of_next < n and chairs[right_of_next] == '#':
                                chairs[right_of_next] = 'C'
                            # Try to sit to the left of next_person
                            elif next_person > 0 and chairs[next_person - 1] == '#':
                                chairs[next_person - 1] = 'C'
                        else:
                            # No next person, sit in middle
                            if n % 2 == 1:
                                middle = n // 2 + 1
                                if middle < n:
                                    chairs[middle - 1] = 'C'
                            else:
                                middle = (n + 1) // 2
                                if middle < n:
                                    chairs[middle - 1] = 'C'
            elif country == 'D':
                # D country does not want to sit next to a person
                # Find the position with minimum distance to closest occupied person
                # If multiple with same distance, choose leftmost
                # If the min distance is 1 (adjacent), then choose leftmost such adjacent position
                # Otherwise, choose the position with min distance
                
                # First find occupied positions
                occupied = []
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] != '#':
                        occupied.append(i)
                
                if len(occupied) == 0:
                    # No one sitting, sit at leftmost
                    for i in range(n):
                        if chairs[i] == '#':
                            chairs[i] = 'D'
                            break
                else:
                    # Find all empty chairs
                    empty_chairs = []
                    for i in range(n):
                        if chairs[i] == '#':
                            empty_chairs.append(i)
                    
                    # For each empty chair, find min distance to any occupied
                    min_dist = float('inf')
                    best_pos = -1
                    
                    for pos in empty_chairs:
                        min_dist_to_pos = min(abs(pos - occ) for occ in occupied)
                        if min_dist_to_pos < min_dist:
                            min_dist = min_dist_to_pos
                            best_pos = pos
                        elif min_dist_to_pos == min_dist and pos < best_pos:
                            best_pos = pos
                    
                    # Now check if best_pos is adjacent to anyone
                    # If it is, then we need to choose the leftmost such adjacent position
                    # But the problem says D doesn't want to sit next to a person
                    # So maybe we only consider non-adjacent positions?
                    
                    # Let's try to find a position that is not adjacent to any occupied
                    # But from example, this seems not to be the case
                    
                    # Given the examples, I'll go with the existing rule
                    chairs[best_pos] = 'D'
        
        print(''.join(chairs))

solve()
```

```python
while True:
    line = input().strip()
    if line == "0 0":
        break
        
    n, m = map(int, line.split())
    passengers = []
    for _ in range(m):
        passengers.append(input().strip())
    
    # chairs: 0 to n-1, initially all empty
    chairs = ['#'] * n
    
    for country in passengers:
        if country == 'A':
            # Sit at leftmost empty chair
            for i in range(n):
                if chairs[i] == '#':
                    chairs[i] = 'A'
                    break
        elif country == 'B':
            # Sit at rightmost empty chair, unless the chair next to A is vacant
            # First, check if there's an A already
            has_A = False
            a_pos = -1
            for i in range(n):
                if chairs[i] == 'A':
                    has_A = True
                    a_pos = i
                    break
            
            # Find all empty chairs
            empty_chairs = []
            for i in range(n):
                if chairs[i] == '#':
                    empty_chairs.append(i)
            
            if has_A and a_pos != -1:
                # Check if the chair immediately to the right of A is empty
                right_of_A = a_pos + 1
                if right_of_A < n and chairs[right_of_A] == '#':
                    # If right of A is empty, sit at leftmost empty chair
                    for i in range(n):
                        if chairs[i] == '#':
                            chairs[i] = 'B'
                            break
                else:
                    # Sit at rightmost empty chair
                    for i in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                        if chairs[i] == '#':
                            chairs[i] = 'B'
                            break
            else:
                # No A, or right of A is not empty, sit at rightmost empty chair
                for i in range(n-1, -1, -1):
                    if chairs[i] == '#':
                        chairs[i] = 'B'
                        break
        elif country == 'C':
            # Try to sit next to someone
            # Find all occupied positions
            occupied = []
            for i in range(n):
                if chairs[i] != '#':
                    occupied.append(i)
            
            if len(occupied) == 0:
                # No one sitting, sit in middle
                if n % 2 == 1:
                    middle = n // 2 + 1
                    if middle < n:
                        chairs[middle - 1] = 'C'
                else:
                    middle = (n + 1) // 2
                    if middle < n:
                        chairs[middle - 1] = 'C'
            else:
                # Find the leftmost occupied position
                leftmost_occupied = min(occupied)
                
                # Try to sit to the right of leftmost_occupied
                right_pos = leftmost_occupied + 1
                if right_pos < n and chairs[right_pos] == '#':
                    chairs[right_pos] = 'C'
                # Try to sit to the left of leftmost_occupied
                elif leftmost_occupied > 0 and chairs[leftmost_occupied - 1] == '#':
                    chairs[leftmost_occupied - 1] = 'C'
                # Try with next person (the next person to the right of leftmost_occupied)
                else:
                    # Find next person (next to the right of leftmost_occupied)
                    next_person = None
                    for pos in occupied:
                        if pos > leftmost_occupied:
                            next_person = pos
                            break
                    if next_person is not None:
                        # Try to sit to the right of next_person
                        right_of_next = next_person + 1
                        if right_of_next < n and chairs[right_of_next] == '#':
                            chairs[right_of_next] = 'C'
                        # Try to sit to the left of next_person
                        elif next_person > 0 and chairs[next_person - 1] == '#':
                            chairs[next_person - 1] = 'C'
                    else:
                        # No next person, sit in middle
                        if n % 2 == 1:
                            middle = n // 2 + 1
                            if middle < n:
                                chairs[middle - 1] = 'C'
                        else:
                            middle = (n + 1) // 2
                            if middle < n:
                                chairs[middle - 1] = 'C'
        elif country == 'D':
            # Find the position with minimum distance to any occupied person
            # If multiple with same distance, choose leftmost
            # If the minimum distance is 1 (adjacent), then choose leftmost such adjacent position
            # Otherwise, choose the position with minimum distance
            
            occupied = []
            for i in range(n):
                if chairs[i] != '#':
                    occupied.append(i)
            
            if len(occupied) == 0:
                # No one sitting, sit at leftmost
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] == '#':
                        chairs[i] = 'D'
                        break
            else:
                # For each empty chair, compute min distance to occupied
                empty_chairs = []
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] == '#':
                        empty_chairs.append(i)
                
                min_dist = float('inf')
                best_pos = -1
                
                for pos in empty_chairs:
                    min_dist_to_pos = min(abs(pos - occ) for occ in occupied)
                    if min_dist_to_pos < min_dist:
                        min_dist = min_dist_to_pos
                        best_pos = pos
                    elif min_dist_to_pos == min_dist and pos < best_pos:
                        best_pos = pos
                
                chairs[best_pos] = 'D'
    
    print(''.join(chairs))
```

You are an expert Python programmer. You will be given a question (problem specification) and will generate a correct Python program that matches the specification and passes all tests.

Solve the following coding problem using the programming language python:

The neutral city of Eyes City, in the heart of the four countries, is home to the transcontinental train Bandai. The platform has a row of chairs for passengers waiting for the Bandai, and people who enter the platform can use the chairs freely.

The Bandai is cheap, fast and comfortable, so there are a constant number of users from the four surrounding countries. Today is the anniversary of the opening, so I'm thinking of doing something special for the people sitting at home. To do this, you need to know where the people who pass through the ticket gate are sitting. Consider the difficult personalities of people from four countries and create a program that simulates how the chairs are buried. People who pass through the ticket gate sit in a row of chairs one after another. People from four countries have their own personalities and ways of sitting. Each sitting style is as follows.

Personality of Country A | Country A should be able to sit down. Look from the left end and sit in an empty chair.

<image>

Personality of country B | Country B is not good at country A. A Sit in an empty chair from the right end, except next to a national. However, if only the person next to Country A is vacant, be patient and sit in the vacant chair from the left end.

<image> <image>

If it is also filled, try to sit next to the next person under the same conditions. If no one is sitting in any of the chairs, sit in the middle chair (n / 2 + 1 if the number of chairs n is odd (n + 1) / 2).
--- | ---
<image> <image>

Personality of D country | D country does not want to sit next to a person. Trying to sit in the chair that is the closest to the closest person. View Full Response 
I'll solve this step by step.

## Problem Understanding

1. We have a row of chairs (n chairs) and passengers entering one by one
2. Each passenger from different countries has specific sitting rules:
   - Country A: sits at the leftmost empty chair
   - Country B: sits at the rightmost empty chair, EXCEPT if only the chair next to A is empty, then sits at leftmost empty chair
   - Country C: wants to sit next to someone. First tries right of leftmost person, then left of leftmost person, then next person, etc. If no adjacent spot, sits in middle
   - Country D: wants to sit as far from others as possible. If multiple options, choose leftmost. If no one, sits at leftmost

3. We need to output the final seating arrangement with '#' for empty chairs

## Step-by-Step Solution

### Step 1: Understand the rules in detail

- **Country A**: Always sits at the leftmost empty chair
- **Country B**: 
  - Normally sits at rightmost empty chair
  - Exception: if only the chair next to A is empty, sits at leftmost empty chair
- **Country C**: 
  - Try to sit next to someone (right of leftmost person, then left of leftmost person, etc.)
  - If no adjacent spot, sit in middle (n//2 + 1 if odd, (n+1)//2 if even)
- **Country D**: 
  - Try to sit as far from others as possible
  - If multiple options with same distance, choose leftmost
  - If no one sitting, sit at leftmost

### Step 2: Implementation approach

1. Initialize an array of chairs (empty initially)
2. For each passenger in order:
   - Find valid position based on nationality
   - Place the passenger there
3. Output the final arrangement

### Step 3: Handle edge cases

- For Country B: need to check if the only empty chair next to A exists
- For Country C: need to check adjacent positions systematically
- For Country D: need to calculate distances to occupied chairs

Let me implement this:

```python
def solve():
    while True:
        line = input().strip()
        if line == "0 0":
            break
            
        n, m = map(int, line.split())
        passengers = []
        for _ in range(m):
            passengers.append(input().strip())
        
        # Initialize chairs as empty (0 means empty, 1 means occupied)
        chairs = ['#'] * n
        
        for nationality in passengers:
            if nationality == 'A':
                # Sit at leftmost empty chair
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] == '#':
                        chairs[i] = 'A'
                        break
                        
            elif nationality == 'B':
                # Sit at rightmost empty chair, except if only next to A is empty
                # First find all empty chairs
                empty_chairs = [i for i in range(n) if chairs[i] == '#']
                
                # Check if there's only one empty chair and it's next to A
                # We need to see if A is seated and the chair next to A is empty
                has_a = False
                a_position = -1
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] == 'A':
                        has_a = True
                        a_position = i
                        break
                
                # Find all empty chairs
                empty_chairs = [i for i in range(n) if chairs[i] == '#']
                
                if len(empty_chairs) > 0:
                    # Normally sit at rightmost
                    position = empty_chairs[-1]
                    
                    # Check if we have only one empty chair and it's adjacent to A
                    # Actually, the rule says: "if only the person next to Country A is vacant, be patient and sit in the vacant chair from the left end"
                    # This seems to mean: if the only empty chair is adjacent to an A person
                    if has_a and a_position != -1:
                        # Check if the empty chair is adjacent to A
                        adjacent_to_a = []
                        for pos in empty_chairs:
                            if pos == a_position - 1 or pos == a_position + 1:
                                adjacent_to_a.append(pos)
                        
                        # If there's only one empty chair and it's adjacent to A, sit at leftmost
                        if len(empty_chairs) == 1 and len(adjacent_to_a) == 1:
                            position = empty_chairs[0]  # This is actually the only empty chair
                        else:
                            # Otherwise sit at rightmost
                            position = empty_chairs[-1]
                    else:
                        # Sit at rightmost
                        position = empty_chairs[-1]
                        
                    chairs[position] = 'B'
                        
            elif nationality == 'C':
                # Try to sit next to someone
                # Look from left side: try right of leftmost person, then left of leftmost person, etc.
                
                # Find occupied positions
                occupied = [i for i in range(n) if chairs[i] != '#']
                
                # If no one is sitting, sit in middle
                if len(occupied) == 0:
                    if n % 2 == 1:
                        position = n // 2
                    else:
                        position = n // 2
                    chairs[position] = 'C'
                else:
                    # Try to find adjacent positions to existing people
                    # Start with the leftmost person
                    leftmost_person = occupied[0]
                    
                    # Try to sit to the right of the leftmost person
                    right_adjacent = leftmost_person + 1
                    if right_adjacent < n and chairs[right_adjacent] == '#':
                        chairs[right_adjacent] = 'C'
                        continue
                        
                    # Try to sit to the left of the leftmost person
                    left_adjacent = leftmost_person - 1
                    if left_adjacent >= 0 and chairs[left_adjacent] == '#':
                        chairs[left_adjacent] = 'C'
                        continue
                        
                    # Try with next person (if any)
                    for pos in occupied[1:]:
                        # Try right of this person
                        right_adjacent = pos + 1
                        if right_adjacent < n and chairs[right_adjacent] == '#':
                            chairs[right_adjacent] = 'C'
                            break
                        # Try left of this person
                        left_adjacent = pos - 1
                        if left_adjacent >= 0 and chairs[left_adjacent] == '#':
                            chairs[pos - 1] = 'C'
                            break
                    
                    # If no adjacent spot found, sit in middle
                    if not any(chairs[i] == 'C' for i in range(n)):
                        if n % 2 == 1:
                            position = n // 2
                        else:
                            position = n // 2
                        chairs[position] = 'C'
                        
            elif nationality == 'D':
                # Try to sit as far from others as possible
                # Calculate distance to each empty chair
                empty_chairs = [i for i in range(n) if chairs[i] == '#']
                
                if len(empty_chairs) == 0:
                    # No empty chairs - shouldn't happen since m <= n
                    pass
                else:
                    # For each empty chair, calculate minimum distance to any occupied chair
                    distances = []
                    for empty_pos in empty_chairs:
                        min_dist = float('inf')
                        for occupied_pos in range(n):
                            if chairs[occupied_pos] != '#':
                                dist = abs(empty_pos - occupied_pos)
                                min_dist = min(min_dist, dist)
                        distances.append((min_dist, empty_pos))
                    
                    # Sort by distance (ascending), then by position (ascending)
                    distances.sort()
                    
                    # Take the one with minimum distance, and if tie, take leftmost
                    position = distances[0][1]
                    chairs[position] = 'D'
        
        # Output the result
        print(''.join(chairs))

solve()
```

```python
def solve():
    while True:
        line = input().strip()
        if line == "0 0":
            break
            
        n, m = map(int, line.split())
        passengers = []
        for _ in range(m):
            passengers.append(input().strip())
        
        # Initialize chairs as empty (representing '#' in output)
        chairs = ['#'] * n
        
        for nationality in passengers:
            if nationality == 'A':
                # Sit at leftmost empty chair
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] == '#':
                        chairs[i] = 'A'
                        break
                        
            elif nationality == 'B':
                # Sit at rightmost empty chair, except if only the chair next to A is empty, then sit at leftmost
                empty_chairs = [i for i in range(n) if chairs[i] == '#']
                
                # Check if there's a person A already seated
                has_a = False
                a_position = -1
                for i in range(n):
                    if chairs[i] == 'A':
                        has_a = True
                        a_position = i
                        break
                
                if len(empty_chairs) > 0:
                    # Normally sit at rightmost
                    position = empty_chairs[-1]
                    
                    # Check if only one empty chair and it's adjacent to A
                    if has_a and a_position != -1:
                        # Check if the empty chair is adjacent to A
                        adjacent_to_a = []
                        for pos in empty_chairs:
                            if pos == a_position - 1 or pos == a_position + 1:
                                adjacent_to_a.append(pos)
                        
                        # If there's only one empty chair and it's adjacent to A, sit at leftmost
                        if len(empty_chairs) == 1 and len(adjacent_to_a) == 1:
                            position = empty_chairs[0]  # The only empty chair
                        else:
                            # Otherwise sit at rightmost
                            position = empty_chairs[-1]
                    else:
                        # Sit at rightmost
                        position = empty_chairs[-1]
                        
                    chairs[position] = 'B'
                        
            elif nationality == 'C':
                # Try to sit next to someone
                occupied = [i for i in range(n) if chairs[i] != '#']
                
                if len(occupied) == 0:
                    # No one sitting - sit in middle
                    if n % 2 == 1:
                        position = n // 2
                    else:
                        position = n // 2  # (n+1)//2 would be n//2 + 1, but let's use n//2 + 1 for odd
                        # According to problem: n/2 + 1 if odd, (n+1)/2 if even
                        # Actually, (n+1)//2 for both gives correct result
                        position = (n + 1) // 2 - 1 if n % 2 == 1 else (n + 1) // 2 - 1
                        # Let me recalculate
                        if n % 2 == 1:
                            position = n // 2
                        else:
                            position = n // 2
                        # The problem says: n/2 + 1 if odd (n+1)/2, so for odd n: (n+1)/2
                        # For even n: (n+1)/2
                        # So it's (n+1)//2 - 1?
                        position = (n + 1) // 2 - 1
                    chairs[position] = 'C'
                else:
                    # Try to find adjacent positions
                    # Start with the leftmost person
                    leftmost = occupied[0]
                    
                    # Try right of leftmost person
                    right_pos = leftmost + 1
                    if right_pos < n and chairs[right_pos] == '#':
                        chairs[right_pos] = 'C'
                        continue
                    
                    # Try left of leftmost person
                    left_pos = leftmost - 1
                    if left_pos >= 0 and chairs[left_pos] == '#':
                        chairs[left_pos] = 'C'
                        continue
                    
                    # Try with next person
                    for pos in occupied[1:]:
                        # Try right of this person
                        right_pos = pos + 1
                        if right_pos < n and chairs[right_pos] == '#':
                            chairs[right_pos] = 'C'
                            break
                        # Try left of this person
                        left_pos = pos - 1
                        if left_pos >= 0 and chairs[left_pos] == '#':
                            chairs[left_pos] = 'C'
                            break
                    
                    # If no adjacent spot found, sit in middle
                    if not any(chairs[i] == 'C' for i in range(n)):
                        position = (n + 1) // 2 - 1
                        chairs[position] = 'C'
                        
            elif nationality == 'D':
                # Try to sit as far from others as possible
                empty_chairs = [i for i in range(n) if chairs[i] == '#']
                
                if len(empty_chairs) == 0:
                    continue
                    
                # Calculate distance to each empty chair from occupied positions
                distances = []
                for empty_pos in empty_chairs:
                    min_dist = float('inf')
                    for occupied_pos in range(n):
                        if chairs[occupied_pos] != '#':
                            dist = abs(empty_pos - occupied_pos)
                            min_dist = min(min_dist, dist)
                    distances.append((min_dist, empty_pos))
                
                # Sort by distance (ascending), then by position (ascending)
                distances.sort()
                
                # Take the one with minimum distance (and leftmost if tie)
                position = distances[0][1]
                chairs[position] = 'D'
        
        # Output the result
        print(''.join(chairs))
```
